Full reboots, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, do not count either unless otherwise specified. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.10. I of course will only have one Blitz! at a time, but when one finishes, I'll try to have another ready to replace it right away... til I run out of ideas. RULES: UPDATED/REWORDED1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.4. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.5. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.6. Games may be repeated.7. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.8. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.9. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.10. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.11. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.12. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.13. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.14. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. I've added and clarified it a bit, so, if you've got a minute, give it a peruse and let me know if you find anything else to be unclear or have any suggestions as to wording. Thanks. New Blitz! is up. This one will last a while since there are 40 shots! It's still 2 guesses per person per day. The theme this time is Capcom. Have fun! I'll be trying something new here. One, there will always be two questions that need a title and system guess up. I used to limit myself to three questions until the old questions were clear, but there's really no reason for that. Two, I will do my best to consistently keep a Blitz! up of at least ten questions. I of course will only have one Blitz! at a time, but when one finishes, I'll try to have another ready to replace it right away... til I run out of ideas. RULES: UPDATED1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. When answering, you must specify which question you are answering with its number. For Example: Q1: The answer is Super Mario Bros. for NES.16. I am not infallible. If I mess something up, PM me and I'll fix it.INCENTIVE/PRIZES: FREE STUFF!Since I am not a forum official, I cannot offer GPs. There are other incentives though. New Blitz! is up. This one will last a while since there are 40 shots! It's still 2 guesses per person per day. The theme this time is Capcom. Have fun! I'll be trying something new here. One, there will always be two questions that need a title and system guess up. I used to limit myself to three questions until the old questions were clear, but there's really no reason for that. Two, I will do my best to consistently keep a Blitz! up of at least ten questions. I of course will only have one Blitz! at a time, but when one finishes, I'll try to have another ready to replace it right away... til I run out of ideas. RULES: UPDATED1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15. It's a Mega Man Blitz! with a twist, so be sure to read the special rules. Still only two guesses per person per day, but no need to guess the system. RULES: 1. Obey the forum's posting rules.2. One game title answer per person per question. If you get something wrong and I correct you, you are allowed to guess again. Once for each time I correct you.3. You can answer both of the non-Blitz! questions in one post. You can answer only two Blitz! questions per day. That makes a total of 4 questions and 8 points possible. If there are Extra Questions posted, you can get even more points by answering as many of those as you'd like.4. Each Blitz! has it's own rules, but I want to reiterate some of the common rules here. Two Blitz! questions, per person, per day. If you are corrected, you may guess again within that day, but only at the two you had initially guessed at. If you pick a question as one of your two that only had the system guess open, it still counts as one of your two questions despite it only being worth 1 point.5. If a question goes unanswered for a certain amount of time or guesses (at my discretion) I will post a new question with the old one in an EXTRA QUESTIONS section.6. Titles are worth one point, a correct system guess is worth 1 bonus point. Extra Questions vary.7. If you change your display name, PM me or include a note in your post along with your next guess and I'll update the scoreboard.8. Games may be repeated.9. As I live in North America, the screens will be of a North American release unless it is unavailable in North America or unless otherwise specified.10. Spelling is at my discretion. If I feel it is obvious that what you typed was intended to be what the correct answer is, points will be rewarded. If I have no idea if it was even in English, then you need to take a typing class.11. I do not include re-releases on WiiWare (Virtual Console), PSN, Steam, etc. as a separate console. For example, Final Fantasy: Mystic Quest was re-released as a download on Wii, but I will not post the Wii version.12. I will do my best to not post full reboots/remakes, such as Final Fantasy 3 on DS, without noting that it is a reboot/remake.13. Arcade is a "console". I also do not count PC/Mac/Linux as separate consoles.14. DS/3DS games may include both screens or just one of the screens at my discretion and without warning.15.
